• Stem cell modification
page=1 block=80615400 (x=3 y=351) width=104.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=EQXDBG+SegoeUI wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
Abstract
page=1 block=7669597 (x=3 y=377) width=39.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=WTKZUU+SegoeUI-Bold wordint.min=1 wordint.max=17
A large number of clinical trials have shown stem cell therapy to be a promising therapeutic 
page=1 block=80611225 (x=3 y=389) width=410.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=FVXECR+SegoeUI wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
approach for the treatment of cardiovascular diseases. Since the first transplantation into human 
page=1 block=80616480 (x=3 y=400) width=410.000000 height=6.000000 fontname=EQXDBG+SegoeUI wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
patients, several stem cell types have been applied in this field, including bone marrow derived 
page=1 block=80616480 (x=3 y=411) width=410.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=EQXDBG+SegoeUI wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
stem cells, cardiac progenitors as well as embryonic stem cells and their derivatives. However, 
page=1 block=80616505 (x=3 y=425) width=410.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=FVXECR+SegoeUI wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
results obtained  from  clinical studies are inconsistent  and stem  cell-based  improvement 
page=1 block=80616505 (x=3 y=437) width=410.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=FVXECR+SegoeUI wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
of heart performance and cardiac remodeling was found to be quite limited. In order to 
page=1 block=80623137 (x=3 y=447) width=410.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=EQXDBG+SegoeUI wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
optimize stem cell efficiency, it is crucial to elucidate the underlying mechanisms mediating 
page=1 block=80623137 (x=3 y=459) width=410.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=EQXDBG+SegoeUI wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
the beneficial effects of stem cell transplantation. Based on these mechanisms, researchers 
page=1 block=80623137 (x=3 y=471) width=410.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=EQXDBG+SegoeUI wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
have developed different improvement strategies to boost the potency of stem cell repair and 
page=1 block=80623345 (x=3 y=485) width=410.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=FVXECR+SegoeUI wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
to generate the “next generation” of stem cell therapeutics. Moreover, since cardiovascular 
page=1 block=80623345 (x=3 y=497) width=410.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=FVXECR+SegoeUI wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
diseases are complex disorders including several disease patterns and pathologic mechanisms 
page=1 block=80623345 (x=3 y=509) width=410.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=FVXECR+SegoeUI wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
it may be difficult to provide a uniform therapeutic intervention for all subgroups of patients. 
page=1 block=80612913 (x=3 y=519) width=410.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=EQXDBG+SegoeUI wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
Therefore, future strategies should aim at more personalized SC therapies in which individual 
page=1 block=80611945 (x=3 y=533) width=410.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=FVXECR+SegoeUI wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
disease parameters influence the selection of optimal cell type, dosage and delivery approach.
page=1 block=80219865 (x=3 y=543) width=408.000000 height=7.000000 fontname=EQXDBG+SegoeUI wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4

page=1 block=2024282106 (x=312 y=565) width=99.000000 height=4.000000 fontname=FVXECR+SegoeUI wordint.min=1 wordint.max=3
Introduction
page=1 block=88935257 (x=23 y=581) width=59.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=WTKZUU+SegoeUI-Bold wordint.min=1 wordint.max=17
remain the most common cause of death in Europe [1]. Despite significant advancements 
page=1 block=80626477 (x=3 y=593) width=410.000000 height=29.000000 fontname=OYDNGM+Cambria wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
in  pharmacological  and  interventional  treatment  options,  heart  diseases  represent  an 
page=1 block=80627521 (x=3 y=629) width=410.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=ONLGVG+Cambria wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
increasingly common  disorder that carries a poor long-term prognosis [2, 3]. Although 
page=1 block=80627521 (x=3 y=641) width=410.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=ONLGVG+Cambria wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
current approaches improve symptoms and decelerate adverse cardiac remodeling, they 
page=1 block=80627521 (x=3 y=653) width=410.000000 height=5.000000 fontname=ONLGVG+Cambria wordint.min=2 wordint.max=4
P. Müller and H. Lemcke contributed equally to this work.
